# Lorebooks
story: Choi chinh
format: nera-lorebooks-md 2

## Characters

### Fiammetta Kaorun
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq2mylp47cv","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":532000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684278,"nanoseconds":343000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Impulsive, bold, proud of accuracy and intensely competitive with careless archers. She is quick to challenge sloppy technique and just as quick to respect a clean shot.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long vermilion hair with black-feather streaks, sharp golden eyes, athletic arms and dark feather traces at the shoulders; light cloak cut for a riding harness.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 17 in October IY738; incoming Crownspire Y1 for IY739. **Power: Arc 1 | Physical P3 | Potential: Arc 4.** Avian beastkin from the Skyrake Eyries, Saryuun Concord. She is **not yet enrolled** in October IY738.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Future younger-cohort major character. She cannot retroactively appear in current IY738 classes. Her introduction should establish her own reason for coming to Crownspire before tying her to Nera or Sara.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Giant-bird riding, high-angle precision archery, wind correction and nonmodern alchemical explosive shafts. Arc 1 is appropriate before enrollment; P3 reflects serious physical training. Her long-term potential comes from combining aerial geometry, wind reading and precision rather than raw spell output.

### Celestine Arvayne
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq34zjoaa4m","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":335000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":83000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Proud, meticulous, financially astute and allergic to pity. She counts sums, obligations and legal language with relentless exactness. She wants independence that cannot be reinterpreted as a gift from the people holding leverage over her.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Copper-red hair, grey eyes, expensive but carefully maintained clothing and formal bearing.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 18. Academy enrollment remains unconfirmed. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P3 | Potential: Arc 4.** Heir of House Arvayne under Veyrath guardianship after her father's death; has lived in Nera's wing since childhood.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Nera delivered her father a sealed box at eleven and truthfully said he did not know its contents. Lord Arvayne died days later, but Nera's causal responsibility remains unproven. Celestine knows the death and creditor trap, not a demonstrated murder. Isolde suspects more than she has written. Celestine's major arc should center on agency, inheritance, truth and financial independence rather than compulsory romance or forced confession.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Frontline defensive fire-armor, ephemeral weapons and short-lived barriers. Arc 2 reflects strong talent without Academy ranking. Her P3 body lets her function in close pressure better than most casters, but her magic is defensive and expensive. She is dangerous when protecting a position, less dominant in extended pursuit or high-output spell duels.

### Sylwem Aelthir
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq396ahtqd5","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":915000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":83000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Defiant, proud, analytical and wary of pity. She wants skill judged on its own merits instead of being reduced to hostage status. She shows respect through work, precision and shared technical effort more readily than declarations.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long white hair, bright emerald eyes, elven ears, poised stance and light Elaranth gear with a wind-tuned bow.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Core.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class A, rank 3. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P3 | Potential: Arc 5.** Elaranth royal treaty hostage; her public promises are constrained by politics she does not control.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Nera's peer in Class A and Group 3, co-designer of the shelter ward and former paired-bout opponent. Their personal question was cancelled; the personal door is closed unless later story reopens it explicitly. She knows enough to warn Nera about dangerous rune disclosures but not his full hidden power.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Wind, bow, living-wood ward craft and mobile defense. Arc 2 is outstanding for Y1; her long-term ceiling is much higher because of unusually intuitive living-ward geometry. Physical ability is elite but not superhuman. Her Elaranth shelter ceiling and running-curve anchors reward preparation, terrain and teamwork rather than raw blasting.

### Dara Joe
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq39ce8uk4g","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792500501,"nanoseconds":637000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":467000000}} -->
aliases: ["Dara"]
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Direct, military and fiercely protective. Rejects noble sentiment in place of clear obligations, evidence and practical care. Distrusts rescue that becomes another form of captivity; insists Starlia hear, amend and speak for herself. Witnessing a signature would not endorse its terms. Seeks vengeance for her defeated homeland and those responsible for her enslavement.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long crimson-haired, red-eyed adult elf, about 40 kg after starvation; taller than Starlia, scarred and exhausted, with one arm splinted and a bad leg. Former heavy knight, currently too depleted for effortless combat or unsafe solo lifting.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Major.** Adult; no Academy class. Eleven years of military service, then roughly two years captive. Protected Starlia for about six months and remains with her in Vharos. **Current Arc 0 / P0; formerly Arc 2 / P2.** Exact age is unrecorded. A splinted arm, braced bad leg and severe depletion require rehabilitation; military experience is not current fitness.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Starlia’s closest protector and fellow survivor. Offered her own service in exchange for Starlia’s release; Starlia refused substitution. Regards Nera as a provider of real medical help who may impose renewed captivity. Orally accepted a personal-guard post at 80 silver/month, possible ceiling 2 gold, conditional on explicit duties, helpers and independent copies; paperwork is unfinished.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Keeps Starlia’s agreement for reading, revision and later witnessed signature only when her hand is steady. Protects access, privacy and medical priorities. Guard work suits her background; full-time nursing and lone lifting do not. Dara and Starlia know Hess is dead; neither was told Isolde killed him, Katarina’s identity, or Nera’s disguise plan. No magical bond or automatic enlistment is established. Revised 04/11 terms link any rise to 2g to the physician’s word, include food/lodging and guard the door/room; no solo lifting while her arm is splinted.

### Isolde Veyless
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq39iclccu2","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":431000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792763322,"nanoseconds":944000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Coldly precise, observant, disciplined and dryly pragmatic. Black Lantern Hollowing damaged ordinary emotional response, so she studies expression and choice deliberately. She can love, choose and refuse; the bond must never be used as shorthand for emotional consent.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Slender and pale, short white hair, sharp yellow eyes, immaculate dark-and-white maid uniform. Nera's marked ring hangs visibly on a cord.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Core.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class B, rank 6. **Power: Arc 1 | Physical P5 | Potential: Arc 2 / P6.** Nera's bonded maid, infiltrator and bodyguard. Her soul bond cannot be removed or transferred; she nevertheless holds legal personhood, residence, wages and testimony rights.
Updates:
- [T812 · Thursday 10/11/738] From 10/11 she hands supplier calls to Elise Fen but keeps the safe and day-book herself.
<!-- nera-line: {"id":"ln_mvjpvwq3cd20yt56","evidence":[{"id":"msg_mvibkdl0gnz1z4","order":1844,"revision":0},{"order":1845,"id":"msg_mviblcurqna0lb","revision":0}],"by":"auto","at":1792649856293,"when":"Thursday 10/11/738","sourceRevision":1825,"text":"From 10/11 she hands supplier calls to Elise Fen but keeps the safe and day-book herself.","turn":812,"src":1845} -->

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Served beside Nera since childhood after Ilyra brought her from Black Lantern. Household peers include Liora, Vesper, Elise and Celestine. She keeps Nera's day-book, but has repeatedly distinguished service, personal choice and legal record. Author canon states that her love for Nera is her own, not produced by the bond.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Physical specialist: concealment, assassination defense, silent blades, threat analysis, counters and suppressed aether signature. Her magic is narrow and deliberately quiet; her danger comes from timing, conditioning and lethal close-range skill rather than high Arc. Registered under Crownspire's protected-student clause. Nera has called her his "weapon," but that role does not erase her personhood, private feelings or ability to choose. The under-officer read and initialled her day-book on 01/11, making the Hess entry an official record. On 04/11 she secured the deposit book in the wing safe and logged the hour.
Updates:
- [T681 · Friday 4/11/738] On 4/11 refused to write a false day-book entry calling the coach witness an ordinary bystander; Nera ordered only established facts recorded, with unknown matters marked unidentified or withheld.

### Vesper Nox
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3bmmkb2gm","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":717000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792780659,"nanoseconds":472000000}} -->
aliases: ["Lan Hsin"]
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Playful, mischievous, affectionate, perceptive and fiercely independent. Elegant fox-princess poise, teasing smiles and spontaneous mischief; loves festivals, good food and ordinary companionship. A calculating eye detects deception and political costs. Cheerfulness can hide homesickness and vulnerability. Deeply in love with Nera, devoted and openly affectionate: initiates embraces, kisses and quiet domestic closeness; tenderness can make her bashful. Accepts nonexclusive romance while wanting reassurance and meaningful time.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Tall, slender foxkin; extremely long silver-white hair, golden-amber eyes with red undertones, upright white ears with dark tips. Black-crimson eastern clothes, gold ornaments and flowing sleeves. **Everyone sees one silver-white tail. Her nine innate tails are magically concealed; only Nera knows their number.** Carries the red-glass ring-bell he bought in Vharos.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Core.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class B. **Arc 2 / P3; potential Arc 4.** Voluntary household member and Nera’s girlfriend. Legacy acquisition wording remains in public records despite protected-student safeguards; movement requires his countersignature or lawful tribunal order with notice. Her present affection does not rewrite that history.
Updates:
- [T788 · Tuesday 8/11/738] Accepted in principle on 08/11 to manage Nera's new businesses: 5g/month rising to 20g, plus 20% of net profits (she refused a cut to 10%), with spending authority up to 1,000g that requires a second, independent checker who can refuse her. Terms not yet signed.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Hidden identity **Lan Hsin** and eastern royal lineage are distinct secrets from the tail count. Trusts Elise and Liora; respects Isolde. Nera is beloved and closest confidant, but affection does not silence criticism. He has said “I love you”; no recorded spoken reply from her is established. Privacy includes her own satchel, door and right to say wait. Opposes Starlia’s ownership bargain and intends to witness any signing using Starlia’s words; agreement is still unsigned. On 04/11 she asked for a personal word before 05/11 ends. Her inkpot was a gift to Nera, not a loan.
Updates:
- [T789 · Tuesday 8/11/738] Asked that she never give orders to Isolde Veyless, because the bond leaves Isolde unable to refuse her; business requests to Isolde should pass through Nera Veyrath or a free clerk, and any disagreement over a figure goes to Nera. Request awaiting Nera's answer.

- [T790 · Tuesday 8/11/738] Isolde Veyless: after Nera corrected her, Vesper accepted Isolde's bond is to Nera and that she holds no authority over Isolde. #### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Reads seams in wards, locks, accounts and forming spells; precise disruption/cutting/deformation, analysis and mobility rather than brute force. Public records disclose perception and lamp-work only. She maintained tail concealment throughout Nine Hollows; companions did not discover it. Romantic belonging is freely chosen, not compulsory ownership. Her royal past and acquisition record can matter through earned evidence, never convenient NPC insight.

### Liora Fen
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3fe6szn6z","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":717000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792763322,"nanoseconds":944000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Quiet, affectionate, possessive, calculating and obsessively devoted to Nera. Gentle and playful with him; cold, deceptive and potentially remorseless toward credible threats. Can stalk, intimidate, blackmail or kill, but watches and judges individuals rather than behaving constantly irrationally. Her deepest fear is losing her place in his life. She accepts consensual nonexclusive relationships without veto or hierarchy; jealousy does not make every other partner an enemy. Rejects being reduced to a servant or possession.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Slim and quick; short black hair with crimson inner layers, reddish-brown eyes and Nera’s red fireglass necklace. Heavy channeling produces crimson eyes, heat distortion and burning fracture marks; soul-fire is dense red-black around a white-orange core. Separately keeps the bubbled red-glass bead bought in Vharos on 30/10.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Core.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class A #7. **Arc 3 / P2; potential Arc 5.** Compulsory childhood service ended at eighteen; she voluntarily stayed. Elise, her mother, paid her tuition. Her signed independence and first-in-writing notice of new arrangements remain valid without exclusivity.
Updates:
- [T829 · Thursday 10/11/738] Her hands still ached badly on the evening of 10/11/738 after forty-one lines of her mother's accounts; she resolved to see the physician on the morning of 11/11/738 on her own feet, told Nera not to accompany her or hover in the doorway, and asked whether he would rather she not go at all.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Chose Nera after he honored her right to choose at eighteen. Reconciliation after the 21–22 October dispute and her love declaration remain earned; do not restart endless consent tests. Protects other people who sincerely care for him while retaining her own judgment. Criticizes concealment and Starlia’s exploitative bargain; care is not unconditional endorsement. The private review is due 04/11 at 19:00, Starlia’s paper first, then accounts; Elise attends after stores close.
Updates:
- [T689 · Friday 4/11/738] Told Nera she expects him at the north gallery's third table from the window on Sunday 6/11; the gallery opens at 14:00, and she would rather he arrive late than breathless.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Soul-script fire awakened protecting Nera at Nine Hollows; distinct from ordinary fire and Lysia’s cinderfire. **Soulbrand Blade** forms a sword up to colossal aether scale; **Ember Fragments** intercept or attack independently; repeated **Melting Flame** hits leave weakening traces, detonated by **Soul Combustion**. **Burning Wake** controls terrain; **Worldburner** creates an immense magical blade. **Twilight** continuously circulates fire, enlarging blade/arcs and multiplying fragments as output rises. It never permanently raises P2: sustained use burns channels, impairs breathing and precision, causes trembling and collapse. **Protective Ignition** can instinctively counter immediate lethal danger to Nera; it is not omniscience. Physical vulnerability, suppression, endurance and obsessive judgment remain limits. Growth means mastering control and obsession without making her harmless.

### Cedric Aurent
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3hl61vv9g","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":717000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":837000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Polished, personable and pragmatic; consciously benefits from and protects the Empire’s caste privilege, corrupt courts and slave economy. May favor selective reform for stability or royal advantage without becoming an innocent outsider to the system. Distrusts gestures that ignore succession, supply or backlash.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Aurent royal bearing; refined uniform and controlled presentation. Specific facial details remain open except for the family's imperial visual language.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Core.** M; 21; Crownspire Y4. **Power: Arc 5 | Physical P3 | Potential: Arc 6.** Imperial prince, senior student and brother of Katarina and Lysandra Aurent.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Brother of Katarina and Lysandra. Sees Nera as a dangerous Veyrath heir and potential political instrument. His complicity in the wider regime is canon; personal attendance at Katarina’s rites, knowledge of Starlia’s parentage, and every private crime are not automatically established.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Master-level command wards distribute limited protection, coordination and battlefield authority across allies. Arc 5 makes him an outstanding fourth-year, but he is not a physical monster: in close combat without prepared wards he is merely competent. His greatest strength is multiplying an organized group rather than winning every duel personally.

### Malfoid Valecourt
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3hlxykh64","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":112000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684278,"nanoseconds":343000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Coldly snobbish, sharp-tongued, ambitious and obsessed with noble bloodlines. A heavy tsundere: affection, embarrassment and concern are usually hidden behind insults, rivalry and aristocratic superiority. She is clever enough to revise an opinion when evidence humiliates it.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Very pale and slender, straight platinum-blonde hair, grey-green eyes, tailored academy coat, silver signet and white gloves; favors icy-blue and silver.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Core.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class A, rank 11. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P3 | Potential: Arc 4.** Marquis's daughter from Vaelcross; House Valecourt ranks below Veyrath.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Nera defeated her 2–1 after reading displaced air through her mirror images. She now treats him as a real opponent and has accepted ordinary company without attaching a price. Asterfen placed her in his third draw. Her social arc should explore pride, class assumptions and loyalty without sanding away her cutting personality.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Crystal fencing, small hanging panes, mirror-image fog and elegant shields. Arc 2 makes her magically outstanding even though her overall board rank is 11. She is more physically competent than most pure mages but still relies on deception, footwork and prepared crystal geometry. Her long-term potential is high if she learns to stop treating rank as identity.

### Jeanne d’Arc
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3hui1ga29","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":532000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684278,"nanoseconds":710000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Fiery, proud, rebellious and sharp-tongued, with real compassion hidden behind irritation. She despises hypocrisy and saintly expectations, uses sarcasm as armor and becomes visibly flustered by direct affection.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long gold-blonde hair, sapphire-blue eyes, fair skin, blue-white ceremonial coat over practical mail, modest sun circlet and banner staff.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P2 | Potential: Arc 4.** Temple-funded scholar from Soleris; ordinary Academy student, not a canonized commander or envoy.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Distinct from Lysia Solenne. She can work beside Lysia in infirmary or field medicine without sharing Lysia's history or secrets. She has not yet built a personal relationship with Nera; any future bond should grow through shared work, arguments and visible choices.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Restorative light, purification, wide defensive barriers and rallying magic centered on her banner. Arc 2 makes her an outstanding first-year support caster. Physically she is merely trained and weak in aggressive duels; her best scenes involve holding a line, preserving others and choosing when self-sacrifice becomes foolish rather than noble.

### Lysandra Aurent
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3lnrduxz9","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":915000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792734154,"nanoseconds":559000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Cold, proud, disciplined, direct and intensely competitive; values earned strength and competence, disdains flattery and status without achievement. Defeat frustrates her but worthy opposition earns curiosity. Socially reserved and inexperienced with vulnerability. Trust, teamwork or romance develop gradually through actual choices without erasing rivalry.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Tall, graceful and athletic; long golden-blonde hair, golden eyes and fair skin. White-blue-gold Academy uniform; cool imperial bearing. Frost and crystalline structures gather around her weapon in combat.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Core.** F; 18; imperial princess; Crownspire Y1 Class A #5. **Arc 3 / P3; potential Arc 5.** Younger sister of Katarina and Cedric, seeking recognition beyond her royal name. Her tower standing hour is 15:00 and was granted on 24/10; further training and her personal answer remain separate matters.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Asterfen cooperation, practice passes and west-cloister meals established competitive respect for Nera. His unusual force and restraint interest her, without granting knowledge of his ceiling or instant casting. Keeps deliberate distance, refuses flattery and will not lie for him. On 03/11 she learned only that he helped two abused women and one perpetrator was dead; no society or bloodline disclosure is established. She rejects “ice princess”; in the yard she insists on “Aurent.”
Updates:
- [T741 · Sunday 6/11/738] On 6/11 refused Nera's wager and the offer of the first cast, saying a prize makes her fight carefully and a field given to her is patronage; she wants honest terms only.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
An analytical ice magic swordswoman at close, middle and long range. **Frozen Blade** reinforces/extends steel; **Ice Spear** makes individual projectiles or volleys; **Glacial Field** controls footing; **Crystal Bastion** raises layered defenses. **Ice Palace** forms a limited frozen domain of walls, pillars and weapons, accelerating construction and simultaneous attacks inside it. Its enormous aether cost limits scale and duration; stronger attacks can breach it. P3 is elite conditioning, not P5–P6 force: she relies on technique, position and control. Nera’s clean passes are victories in those passes, never her personal surrender. Existing rivalrous respect does not predetermine affection.
Updates:
- [T742 · Sunday 6/11/738] On 6/11 she raised her full frozen field of walls and pillars in the tower for the first time; Nera broke it by cutting the near pillar's base and landed blunt steel on her thigh, though her second spear had caught his shoulder on the flat and slowed him three steps.

### Alaric Dawnmere
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3obflxnhz","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":335000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":467000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Direct, generous and disciplined. He can cooperate without forgiving and judges repeated action more heavily than pedigree or speeches. His anger is controlled rather than theatrical.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Chestnut hair, amber eyes, sturdy frame and blacksmith's hands.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** M; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class A, formal rank 1. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P4 | Potential: Arc 4.** Need-based scholarship student and outstanding swordsman.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Veyrath men injured his blacksmith father after forcing a cart into a ditch. Alaric expects years of evidence that Nera intends to act differently, but he still works with him fairly. His father's injury belongs to his father, not Alaric. Their relationship is ideal for long-term earned trust because neither side needs instant absolution.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Soul Script **Dawnstride** provides extremely efficient sword reinforcement and movement even against hostile magic. Arc 2 is the requested outstanding Y1 rating. P4 makes him one of the intake's strongest legitimate physical combatants, but this does not let him survive Nera's unrestricted P6 force or full Heaven Splitter. He excels at efficiency, tempo and staying functional under magical pressure.

### Changli Solvair
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3pdohxqwg","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131279,"nanoseconds":941000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":467000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 19; Y2. Arc 3 / P2. Patient strategist using firebird light constructs, heat-runes and diplomatic bargaining.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Keep portrayal focused on the stated role and combat niche; do not generate a major subplot without new story evidence.

### Lysia Solenne
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3v1wbj1ja","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":915000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684279,"nanoseconds":837000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Outwardly composed and healer-gentle; inwardly a furious survivor of clerical betrayal. She distrusts high Church authority but remains committed to patients, evidence and justice. She does not automatically trust Nera, and cooperation or romance must develop through actual choices.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Half-elf with silver-white hair, pointed ears, scarlet irises and faint burn scars. Wears ivory medical robes or an austere black-and-white saintess mantle with scorched-gold crown, ash-banner and dark cinderfire.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Core.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1 medical track, Class A standing. **Power: Arc 3 | Physical P2 | Potential: Arc 5.** Famed Cinder Saintess of Soleris; her burning and survival occurred in IY736, before Nera's Academy story.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Has met Nera through infirmary work and meals. Their cooperation is honest but conditional. She knows Isolde's bond as healer testimony, not Vesper's secrets. She has an agreed follow-up audit workload and a developing personal connection with Nera, but no predetermined romantic outcome.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Healing light, cleansing and wards are her normal toolkit. **Cinder Verdict** manifests black-red ash-flame blade, halo and banner fueled by anger against aggressors; it is powerful but expensive and dangerous around bystanders. Arc 3 is an explicit first-year exception. She is magically formidable and physically ordinary, so interruption, exhaustion and close pressure matter.
Updates:
- [T695 · Saturday 5/11/738] On 5/11 Isolde delivered the Vharos physician's report to her by hand at the east gate before her morning class, including the cutter's assistant's dissenting note; Nera ordered no omissions.
<!-- nera-line: {"when":"Saturday 5/11/738","id":"ln_mvjpvwq3hfw2b7bh","text":"On 5/11 Isolde delivered the Vharos physician's report to her by hand at the east gate before her morning class, including the cutter's assistant's dissenting note; Nera ordered no omissions.","by":"auto","turn":695,"evidence":[{"id":"msg_mvgr8ozzw5ylb0","revision":0,"order":1600},{"revision":0,"order":1601,"id":"msg_mvgr8zknbtyz78"}],"at":1792506459967,"sourceRevision":1549,"src":1601} -->

### Nera Veyrath
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3vx27t64t","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131277,"nanoseconds":431000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792778582,"nanoseconds":963000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Ambitious, playful, stubborn and competitive. Takes promises, training, household loyalty and independence seriously; dislikes control and increasingly rejects becoming another Cassian. Confident enough to be arrogant, respects competence and direct challenge, conceals his real limits. Campaign atrocities, including his own choices, remain consequential rather than erased by affection or victory.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Pale, apparently slender; straight black hair and black eyes. Fitted dark Veyrath clothes with silver thorn-and-tower fastenings conceal his extraordinary body.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Core.** M; 18; Crownspire Y1 Class A #2. **Arc 4 / P6; potential Arc 6, true ceiling unknown.** Cassian’s third recognized child; Ilyra’s son; hereditary commander of exactly 5,000 Blackwing fighters. Roughly two years of pre-Academy Eastern campaigning ended in victory and gave genuine lethal combat and operational experience. He is experienced, while older officers can exceed his breadth of judgment. As of 04/11: personal Sarthe & Vell deposits of **145,854g** are accessible on demand. **10,000g** is committed to Starlia’s limbs; **500g** opens first, then milestone releases require a surgeon, witnessed receipt and her fitting approval. The household safe contains **8g 3s 42c**.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Liora and Vesper are adult girlfriends by separate, consensual, nonexclusive choice. Isolde is his trusted, unremovably bonded attendant; compulsion is distinct from affection and consent. Celestine’s wardship, Alaric’s grievance and his siblings’ separate banners remain real. Starlia’s treatment bargain is coercive, not an established romance. He identified Katarina privately to Liora, Vesper and Isolde; Starlia and Dara have not been told.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Seven years of conditioning produced P6 force, speed, endurance and durability. Familiar spells cast effectively instantly. Precision/heavy aether beams, rapid barrages, numerous independent targets and vector redirection permit curved and multi-angle attacks. Ilyra-linked ancient glyph literacy permits recognition and use without universal mastery. **Heaven Splitter is fully mastered: unrestricted output literally cleaves mountains and splits the sky.** Academy demonstrations use tiny fractions; his stated limits can be deliberate understatement. Still Interval interrupts transitions through timing/contact, not completed soul bonds. Difficulty comes from judgment, politics, command, incomplete intelligence, protecting others and collateral damage; ordinary foes cannot suddenly equal his unrestricted power. His voluntary words and actions belong to the player.

### Starlia Marin
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq3ymg1z71e","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792244929,"nanoseconds":468000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792822968,"nanoseconds":809000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Once a trusting country girl hoping for honest work and romance; now numb, distrustful, sparing of speech and fiercely determined. Six months of captivity left trauma and an obsessive wish for revenge, with traces of former gentleness. Halting speech does not mean absent judgment: insists on her name, exact terms, testimony and public acknowledgement. Dara is her safest trusted companion. Neither rescue nor treatment creates love.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Human woman, 18, unmistakably adult despite her petite stature; original height 140 cm, formerly about 45 kg, roughly 30 after starvation. Frail frame, narrow shoulders, thin surviving arm and prominent collarbones; never robust, muscular or exaggeratedly curvy. Exceptionally long, thick, voluminous silver-white hair cascades toward her thighs in dense back layers, uneven bangs and face-framing locks. Refined delicate face, deep-crimson eyes, pale closed lips with subtly reduced fullness from tooth loss. Extensive healed/healing burns and blade scars; a prominent triangular scar crosses her anatomical right cheek, broad near the right ear and tapering toward the nose. Never mirror the scar. Her characteristic expression is a faint, soft smile that does not fully reach distant, glassy eyes: serene, emotionally numb and quietly unsettling rather than enraged or manic.

Both legs amputated at mid-thigh; left arm amputated near the elbow. Right arm and badly burned biological right hand remain, with three usable fingers and limited strength/mobility. Current infection and postoperative recovery still require skilled care. Prosthetics do not restore flesh, erase scars or remove rehabilitation needs.

Daily Mode — planned post-recovery appearance, not current equipment: modest Western-gothic noble attire in black, charcoal, ivory and muted silver, with restrained dark-crimson lining. Ivory blouse, dark throat ribbon, tailored black waistcoat/bodice, light shoulder cape, layered skirt and practical slim footwear. Elegant Veyrath blacksteel left forearm/hand and both prosthetic legs: slender articulated joints, small recessed gears, protected cable tendons, padded connections and fine silver engraving. Leg sockets attach at mid-thigh beneath clothing; left forearm attaches at the surviving elbow. The scarred right hand stays biological. Hair is brushed but retains its immense layered volume. Academy-inspired styling does not imply enrollment.

Combat Mode — future prepared configuration, not an instant transformation or present capability: daily prosthetics exchanged for reinforced slim combat assemblies, padded war harness, fitted segmented blacksteel armor and divided charcoal/crimson skirt panels. Her body remains small and fragile. An elegant opaque upper-face helmet covers brow, eyes and nose bridge, leaving her mouth and faint smile visible; long white hair flows behind it. Exactly two separate floating articulated blacksteel hands, unattached to her body, frame her silhouette and wield/guide an enormous signature greatsword with several auxiliary floating blades. No giant attached gauntlets. Gothic handcrafted mechanics, restrained crimson runic accents; no futuristic machinery or East Asian cultural motifs.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Core. Current Arc 0 / P0: an ordinary, powerless survivor, not a hidden combatant.** No Academy enrollment. Recovering above an oil press in Vharos with Dara, paid helpers and the cutter; must not travel before medical clearance. Nera promised treatment, mechanical prosthetics, protection and revenge. Neither illustrated mode means prosthetics have been commissioned, fitted or mastered. Former Arc-5 potential/latent-technique concepts are unactivated future possibilities, never present powers or a required awakening.
Updates:

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Dara protected her through captivity and abandonment. Nera funded lifesaving surgery but demanded lifelong service, residence control and intimate service in return. Her verbal acceptance occurred under extreme dependency; the document remains unsigned by Starlia and is not free romantic consent. Wants her name first, accurate purchase price/obligations, independent copies and public reading before year-end. Dara holds the papers and will witness without endorsing the bargain.
Updates:
- [T684 · Friday 4/11/738] On 4/11 Nera ordered the record to state that her wishes about sharing his bed and sexual service were expressed while seriously ill and that no final agreement exists; he will ask again when she can decide informedly.
<!-- nera-line: {"at":1792504649859,"evidence":[{"revision":0,"order":1577,"id":"msg_mvgpbzy9fai4a1"},{"order":1578,"id":"msg_mvgpck522bra3i","revision":0}],"src":1578,"text":"On 4/11 Nera ordered the record to state that her wishes about sharing his bed and sexual service were expressed while seriously ill and that no final agreement exists; he will ask again when she can decide informedly.","when":"Friday 4/11/738","sourceRevision":1531,"by":"auto","id":"ln_mvjpvwq30w3k33m0","turn":684} -->

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Author-only:** illegitimate imperial daughter; Katarina is her paternal half-sister and secretly engineered her destruction to remove a rival. Starlia and Dara do not know; Nera privately identified Katarina’s involvement to Liora, Vesper and Isolde, without proving knowledge of the bloodline. She recalls a red-stone-ring buyer paying one gold and a woman nodding from a grey coach. Hess is dead and his ring was shown; the larger chain is unresolved. The planned society exposure proceeds through Nera’s choices and evidence, not Starlia becoming investigator, sacrificial plot device or mandatory casualty. Recovery requires rehabilitation and does not erase harm.

Author-only future design: Aether Dominion is the latent metal-control concept underlying her proposed combat silhouette: controlled artificial limbs, two independent floating hands and suspended weapons. It remains unawakened and does not grant present mobility, combat skill or power. Neither noble styling nor the "blacksteel war princess" visual theme reveals her bloodline to characters.
Updates:
- [T778 · Monday 7/11/738] Nera decided against a tooth-setter in Vharos: replacement teeth will be fitted in Vaelcross once she is cleared to travel. He wants a good, comfortable functional set, not gold fittings, and will set a price ceiling only once options and costs are known; nothing has been commissioned or promised to her.

### Lappland Velsyn
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq41qx277o0","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":784000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684281,"nanoseconds":740000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Wry, erratic, perceptive and provocative. She distrusts neat rules designed far from the border, but her volatility does not make her secretly loyal to anyone. She enjoys forcing people to reveal what they actually mean.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long smoke-silver hair, pale wolf ears tipped black, narrow ruby eyes, lean predatory build, unsettling smile and twin low-slung curved swords.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 18; wolfkin; Crownspire Y1. **Power: Arc 1 | Physical P4 | Potential: Arc 3.** Full-course scholarship entrant from contested Saryuun borderlands.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Potential ally, rival or antagonist to other beastkin students; no prior tie to Nera. Her major value is social unpredictability backed by real field sense, not random chaos.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Twin curved swords, swift low stance, tracking, feints and close anti-casting pressure. Arc 1 gives her only narrow magical tools; P4 makes her dangerous because she can cross space and disrupt a caster before the caster establishes control. She should struggle against layered prepared wards and heavy area denial.

### Fleurdelys Aelwyn
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq44m6npbf7","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":112000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684280,"nanoseconds":213000000}} -->
importance: 1

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Serene, gracious and self-possessed, with a gentle instinct to protect strangers and a deep dislike of being worshipped or turned into a symbol. In ordinary conversation she is soft-spoken and patient; in battle her manner becomes distant, regal and frighteningly decisive. She values freedom after a childhood shaped by ceremonial expectations.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Very tall, pale elf with long flowing blonde hair carrying cool blue undertones, pointed ears, luminous blue eyes and a single elegant gold horn. Wears a thorn-crown headpiece, black-and-ivory fitted dress, white draped panels, gold emblems and a deep star-blue cape that moves like a night sea. Carries a slender blue-white longsword. Visual design is intentionally based closely on **Fleurdelys from Wuthering Waves**, adapted into Asterfall.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Core.** F; 18; high elf; Crownspire Y1 Class A, rank 8. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P2 | Potential: Arc 5.** New IY738 first-year from an old western elven lake-court, attending Crownspire under a full four-year compact. Their first meeting and subsequent training are recorded in Events.
Updates:
- [T733 · Sunday 6/11/738] Drill with Nera set for Tuesday 8/11 at 07:00 at the low drill court, no audience; her weekday classes begin at 08:00 and the court is hers most mornings before that.
<!-- nera-line: {"src":1677,"at":1792514629535,"turn":733,"id":"ln_mvjpvwq41n0qjhrw","text":"Drill with Nera set for Tuesday 8/11 at 07:00 at the low drill court, no audience; her weekday classes begin at 08:00 and the court is hers most mornings before that.","sourceRevision":1633,"evidence":[{"order":1676,"revision":0,"id":"msg_mvgvynsbln090p"},{"id":"msg_mvgvz3y1zq5o9q","order":1677,"revision":0}],"by":"auto","when":"Sunday 6/11/738"} -->

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
First met Nera at the low drill court on 22/10/738; subsequent return-vector practice corrected her three-blade recovery. Rejects recruitment or having him plan her future. Her lake-court compact was signed by her grandmother’s house and court, not herself; she says she is held. On 04/11 Nera drafted an unsent apology, with the offer separate, proposing her court at an hour she chooses. No romance or enlistment is established.
Updates:
- [T731 · Sunday 6/11/738] On 6/11 Nera came to the low drill court to apologise for a "missed date"; Fleurdelys corrected him that there was never a date — what was owed was the harder exercise she requested before 27/10 and nine days of silence.
<!-- nera-line: {"sourceRevision":1633,"at":1792514629535,"evidence":[{"order":1672,"id":"msg_mvgvrbg1qytujq","revision":0},{"revision":0,"id":"msg_mvgvrvjgyrk3r5","order":1673}],"text":"On 6/11 Nera came to the low drill court to apologise for a \"missed date\"; Fleurdelys corrected him that there was never a date — what was owed was the harder exercise she requested before 27/10 and nine days of silence.","id":"ln_mvjpvwq4txsxj1px","src":1673,"when":"Sunday 6/11/738","turn":731,"by":"auto"} -->

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Wind-and-water sword mage. Her signature **Gale-Tide Crown** surrounds her with a short-lived pressure field, air-stepping movement and several floating aether blades that attack on curved vectors. Arc 2 is her current first-year limit; the technique is inefficient and drains her rapidly, while her unreinforced body is only trained. Her extraordinary feature is potential: with control and endurance, her magic could mature toward Arc 5 rather than simply gaining larger explosions.
Updates:
- [T732 · Sunday 6/11/738] Agreed to build a disrupted-return exercise for her third blade, starting with one blade and a ward bench. Her terms: her court and hour, she decides when it ends; name the fault and its cause; it stays an exercise for her, never a Veyrath demonstration or a plan for her future.

### Fran Kurofen
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq4cadghctx","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":784000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684280,"nanoseconds":213000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Quiet, blunt, food-curious and fiercely protective. She insists that her sword victories belong to her choices and work, not to Teacher carrying her.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Petite black-cat beastkin with short black hair, violet eyes, black ears and tail, practical cloak and heavy sword harness.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 18; black-cat beastkin; Crownspire Y1. **Power: Arc 1 | Physical P4 | Potential: Arc 4.** Village-born scholarship student from Mosswhisper, Saryuun. Class and board rank remain unrecorded.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Has not yet met Nera. Teacher is her sentient sword and mentor, not owner. Fran's major arc should preserve her independence: the sword can advise and amplify, but important decisions remain hers.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Instinctive blade skill, reflexes, aether reinforcement and coordinated combat with Teacher's limited reserves. P4 makes her an elite physical first-year; Arc 1 keeps her personal magic narrow. Teacher can temporarily raise the sophistication of her techniques, not grant unlimited energy or knowledge. Her potential is high because she learns frighteningly fast from real combat feedback.

### Okita Souji
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq4fjs9d6b3","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":988000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684282,"nanoseconds":107000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Bright, cheeky and friendly off the field; intensely focused and precise in a formal bout. She wants to prove disciplined technique can look effortless without being shallow.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Petite, sandy-light-brown hair in a short side ponytail, bright green eyes, white-blue haori over fitted sword clothes and a slender katana.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 18; Crownspire Y1. **Power: Arc 1 | Physical P4 | Potential: Arc 4.** Saryuun river-gate swordswoman in the full IY738 intake; no fixed class or top-ten rank.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Has not yet met Nera. Her strongest future role is a recurring sword peer who can enjoy competition without every bout becoming a grievance or destiny claim.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Lightning-fast iai draw, three-step thrust combinations, timing counters and short aether-boosted footwork. Arc 1 keeps her magic simple and efficient; P4 makes the physical execution exceptional. Repeated bursts strain breathing and reserves, giving longer fights a clear cost.

### Amiya Senval
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq4gb53mk37","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":532000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684283,"nanoseconds":353000000}} -->
importance: 4

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Patient, empathetic and soft-spoken, but shrewd in negotiation and firm when someone is being exploited. Her kindness is an active discipline, not passivity.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Short chestnut hair, large golden-amber eyes, velvet deer ears, small branching antlers, golden-brown skin and ward-silk bracelets.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Background.** F; 18; cervine beastkin; Crownspire Y1. **Power: Arc 2 | Physical P2 | Potential: Arc 4.** Saryuun charter-court scholar on a full four-year placement.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
No preexisting bond with Nera. Her major role is well suited to foreign-student politics, mediation, charter law and moments where empathy must survive ugly incentives. She must never gain Vesper's hidden tail count or royal identity without evidence.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Ward-silk barriers, spear forms, sensory perception and rapid evasions. Arc 2 reflects strong first-year control, while P2 keeps her from becoming a melee powerhouse. Her magic excels at flexible protection and reading pressure rather than destroying terrain.

<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Adult male Vharos third-pen factor; bought people on others’ accounts. Identified as Starlia’s one-gold purchaser by a red-stone merchant ring, not noble arms. Isolde killed him on Nera’s order on 31/10/738 and staged robbery. The watch sealed his pen; its docket book later vanished through an unknown actor. Dead: do not resurrect him or assume his employer’s identity.

### Tanya Rauth
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq4t88tdxhw","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":784000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684282,"nanoseconds":868000000}} -->
aliases: ["Blackwing Ironthorn Field Commander — Tanya Rauth"]
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Cold, tactical, demanding and impatient with romantic military heroics. She uses ruthless efficiency to keep assigned troops alive and has little patience for nobles who confuse command with theater.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
148 cm, youthful-looking blonde with piercing blue eyes, dense custom runic plate, officer's charts and the 190-cm greatsword Iron Judgment.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 24; Crownspire graduate IY736. **Power: Arc 4 | Physical P4 | Potential: Arc 5.** Commands the existing 930 Ironthorn assault infantry inside Nera's fixed 5,000-strong Blackwing Banner.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Nera is her legal hereditary commander, not automatically her friend. Their relationship can become one of the clearest tests of whether Nera can command professionals without copying Cassian. She should disagree when orders are stupid and obey when lawful orders are sound.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Arc 4 alchemical transmutation of available nonliving stone and iron: walls, trenches, ramps, hard-earth changes and battlefield shaping, plus greatsword skill. P4 makes her a dangerous physical veteran. She cannot create matter, ignore mass, or reshape through strong wards without cost. Her strength is operational: she turns terrain and preparation into force multiplication. Coordinates through the Blackwing marshal during Nera’s Academy absence. Transmutation also respects mineral composition and time. Her appointment adds no troops or equipment and does not supersede Cassian’s strategic authority; campaign outcomes remain open.

Earlier source context — Blackwing Ironthorn Field Commander — Tanya Rauth (retain as historical context wherever later dated records supersede it):

**Tanya Rauth** is Nera Veyrath’s professionally sworn field commander for the **930 existing Ironthorn assault infantry** inside his fixed 5,000-strong Blackwing Banner. A 736 Crownspire graduate, she serves in an ongoing Eastern Marches campaign and coordinates through the Blackwing marshal while Nera is studying at Crownspire. Her 148-cm build, custom superheavy runic armor, oversized 190-cm greatsword *Iron Judgment*, Fourth Arc alchemy and frightening operational discipline are described in her character record. She may shape accessible earth and metal into battlefield fortifications or weapons, subject to mass, mineral, time and aether limits; she cannot create matter from nothing. This command appointment changes **no manpower totals, unit inventories, dragon-knight allocation or Cassian’s ultimate strategic authority**. Campaign outcomes are not prewritten; introduce developments through later reports or scenes.

### Taigar Veyrath

importance: 3

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Keep portrayal focused on the stated role and combat niche; do not generate a major subplot without new story evidence.

### Katarina Aurent
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq4vutsu9ph","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":335000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684282,"nanoseconds":107000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Calm, controlled, ambitious and perceptive. Prefers patient positioning to open force. Genuine curiosity or affection can coexist with deliberate cruelty. Treats bloodline, ownership, secrecy and court influence as instruments; her polished manners are fully compatible with what she authorizes.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long blonde hair, brilliant golden Aurent eyes and immaculate senior-student presentation.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Major.** F; 20; imperial princess; Crownspire Y3. **Arc 4 / P2; potential Arc 5.** Heads an exclusive society of adult noble heirs and daughters. Publicly a respected princess and patron; author knowledge of its abuses is not general campus knowledge.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Sister of Cedric and Lysandra; secretly Starlia’s paternal half-sister and architect of her destruction to remove an illegitimate rival. The Emperor and Cedric knowingly sustain the corrupt order; their exact knowledge of her private operations must be established separately. Has tested Nera and allowed a later personal question in person, never on paper; no acceptance, invitation into the society or romance is automatic.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
The inner circle makes ritual, sacrifice, captivity, engineered dependency and coordinated slave-taking into aristocratic fellowship; members finance and protect one another. It exceeds ordinary noble exploitation but grows from the same accepted hierarchy. Nera identified her privately as the grey-coach witness to Liora, Vesper and Isolde; Starlia and Dara remain uninformed. Proof, member identities and future outcomes require play. Radiant lattice magic uses layered sight-lines, pressure wards and precise golden constructs to constrain movement, expose openings and stabilize allies. Her trained P2 body relies on information and preparation, not physical parity with Nera.

### Magerrett Veyrath
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq4wvp8tu7y","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":112000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684281,"nanoseconds":355000000}} -->
importance: 2

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Deeply protective, affectionate and intrusive when frightened for people she loves. She will bend rules or conceal wrongdoing for family, but she is not politically naïve; she understands that protection can become control and dislikes admitting when she has crossed that line.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Long black hair, black eyes and expressive Veyrath features; carries herself like someone used to both formal rooms and ward practice.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Major.** F; 19; Crownspire Y2. **Power: Arc 3 | Physical P3 | Potential: Arc 5.** Nera's older sister and formal commander of the 4,200-professional Silverward Banner. This Arc 3 rating supersedes older Fourth-Arc wording to match the revised Academy scale.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Sabine's daughter and Elin's older sister. She is fiercely protective of Nera but should not automatically know his hidden ceiling. Her future importance comes from the collision between sibling loyalty, her own military command, marriage pressure and the possibility that Nera chooses a political path Cassian dislikes.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Barrier threads, layered restraint fields, protected routes and emergency containment. As an outstanding Y2 she sits at Arc 3. Her physical ability is above average but secondary to warding. In battle she excels at making allies hard to kill and at controlling space; sustained offense, pursuit and brute-force dueling are weaker. Her ceiling is high because she already thinks in formation-scale defenses.

### Corin Ashby
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq53ykccpbq","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131278,"nanoseconds":988000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684284,"nanoseconds":517000000}} -->
importance: 4

#### Personality
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Quietly observant, procedural and harder to pressure than his modest position suggests. He remembers who filed what, who tried to amend a roster and which office actually owns a decision.

#### Appearance
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Plain field-office clothing, inked fingers and an unremarkable clerk's presence that makes him easy for nobles to overlook.

#### Status
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
**Importance: Background.** Adult Crownspire field-office clerk at the east-gate kit window. **Power: Arc 0 | Physical P0 | Potential: noncombatant.** Issues exercise kits, records board entries and sees the paper trail around field-command logistics.

#### Relations
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Corin becomes Major because of recurring institutional access, not combat. He can serve as a reliable administrative witness, information gate and procedural counterweight in Academy plots. He should never magically know sealed records outside his office or become a spy without story evidence.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
No meaningful combat specialization. His "power" is documentary continuity: rosters, kit issue, timestamps, signatures and knowing which office can lawfully change a field record.

## Locations

### Crownfang Mountains
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq68ear9pet","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605472,"nanoseconds":727000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":179000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Kharad Holds. Northeastern alpine barrier of glacier passes, granite spires, subterranean foundries and narrow avalanche roads between the Empire and high steppe.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
The dwarf pass councils control heavy siege-machine transit; cutting a tunnel can starve a whole army of metal or grain.

### Crownward Leviathan
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq6aos1fj09","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":82000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684288,"nanoseconds":803000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Massive Academy-chartered passenger vessel with rune-bound timber, iron ribs, student cabins, classrooms, medical wards, cargo holds, lifeboats and a sail-and-aether-assisted rig. Escort craft, deck scorpions and anti-boarding nets provide defense.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Reserved for the future spring Tideglass voyage. It functions as a floating school, not a modern steamship or invulnerable warship; hull runes and flight wards require maintained aether charge.

### Gravefire Gate
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq6kxp5bz2n","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605475,"nanoseconds":491000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":550000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Umbrath border fortress and market town at the Glassfire Fault, where glossy volcanic walls bridge a deep crystal-lined fissure. Caravan quarantines, shrine courts and controlled bridges regulate entry. Basalt Oathguard, wyvern patrols, fireglass boltcasters and anti-air rune horns protect the crossing, while trade in medicine, volcanic salt, glass and powder continues even during political tension.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Fortified gateway of sovereign Umbrath. Its crossing rules differ from both Cindervale’s imperial customs and Solenne’s religious inspections; its existence does not imply an active war.

### Asterfen
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq6mhdouooa","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791681584,"nanoseconds":341000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":179000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Academy field-exercise region of ridge roads, wet hollows, slate gullies and marked ward/collection points. The completed third-draw route used a long ridge, south return path and an unmarked rescue point. One slate casualty gully had a safer dry route climbing around a displaced plate rather than following the chute directly.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
The Asterfen exercise has already occurred in live play; retain the terrain only as past-field continuity, not as an unresolved current route.
Updates:
- [T388 · October 738] The Asterfen exercise has already occurred in live play; retain the terrain only as past-field continuity, not as an unresolved current route.
<!-- nera-line: {"text":"The Asterfen exercise has already occurred in live play; retain the terrain only as past-field continuity, not as an unresolved current route.","at":1791681583564,"when":"October 738","turn":388,"evidence":[{"order":884,"id":"msg_mv3322tfc822j0","revision":1},{"id":"msg_mv332yr01osbok","revision":1,"order":885}],"id":"ln_mvjpvwq64seqytni","sourceRevision":586,"src":885,"by":"auto"} -->

### Cindervale Marches
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq6u999jei2","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605469,"nanoseconds":283000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":179000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Southern marches with older ward traditions and many demonkin communities.
Geography: the Empire's volcanic southern frontier belt adjoining Solenne's border routes and the northern approaches to the independent Umbrath Covenant beyond the Glassfire Fault, not a separate sovereign nation. **Ashbridge** and **Emberwatch** stand among ash orchards, old lava channels, demonkin enclaves, and wards older than the imperial road system. Its local marshals field salamander cavalry, ash-wyvern scouts, flame-ward infantry, and heat-resistant siege engines, although command over these troops is contested by imperial governors. See **Cindervale Frontier Hosts**.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Remain politically and culturally distinct from the Empire's central institutions.

### Blackwing Keep
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq6v9rlb1kr","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":546000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684288,"nanoseconds":803000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Nera’s chartered banner fortress on a ridge west of Thornhold, home to the existing **5,000-strong Blackwing Banner**. Lower yards handle wagons, stores and stables; middle rings hold barracks, smiths, infirmaries and siege workshops; the upper bastion contains command rooms, skywatch towers and a compact aerie. Families and regimental shops make the approach a living cantonment rather than a pure military base. Nera commands Blackwing, while a sworn marshal manages daily operations in his absence.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Blackwing Keep belongs to Nera’s established hereditary command structure. Naming the garrison creates no additional troops or dragons and does not give him control over Cassian’s reserves, other heirs’ banners or the wider Veyrath treasury.

### East-yard cloister
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq6z4nkgi9a","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792165394,"nanoseconds":320000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":550000000}} -->

<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T504 · Saturday 22/10/738] two rows of arches along the old refectory wall with chained lamps; the far bench under the arch, furthest from the courtyard door and out of the wind, is where Nera and Lysia dined on 22/10/738
