### Refuse slope below the third terrace
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq72e5xj7vy","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792302340,"nanoseconds":810000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":964000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T549 · Sunday 30/10/738] outer slope beyond the holding row in Vharos's west basin where discarded bonded people are left among ash, offal, broken crates and cold
<!-- nera-line: {"id":"ln_mvjpvwq7hnrapq3q","by":"auto","sourceRevision":1171,"when":"Sunday 30/10/738","at":1792302340283,"text":"outer slope beyond the holding row in Vharos's west basin where discarded bonded people are left among ash, offal, broken crates and cold","evidence":[{"id":"msg_mvchq2amk42nmk","revision":0,"order":1275},{"order":1276,"revision":1,"id":"msg_mvchrhe0w0itei"}],"turn":549,"src":1276} -->

### Kharzun
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq73bwdmr6q","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605471,"nanoseconds":767000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":856000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Varkesh capital on an iron-rich eastern mesa. Red-brick furnace districts, winter granaries, artillery yards and stepped walls surround an austere palace and bear-mounted guard quarters. Miners and grain traders hold political weight beside frontier nobles, while the city’s military strength depends heavily on distant food routes and caravan treaties.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Officers and factions disagree over rationing, imports and frontier policy; Varkesh is not politically unanimous about war.

### Forgevault
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq75omo86mx","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605470,"nanoseconds":847000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":935000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Fortified Veyrath foundry city built into a jagged ore canyon. Kiln-red forge towers, shielded manufactories, ore tunnels, water-driven hammers and blacksteel aerial screens produce armor plate, siege engines, counter-ward shells and repair stock. Districts such as the Veinworks, Red Furnace, Hammerward and Powder Houses are separated by blast shutters. Skilled smith families, coerced industrial labor and dangerous aether or powder work coexist; despite its output, Forgevault still depends on protected food, timber, charcoal and reagent routes.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Primary metallurgical supply center for the Veyrath warhost. Security is severe and includes patrols, roll calls and hidden detention spaces; its strength depends on continued raw-material and food supply.

### Luneshar
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq78pv4v15p","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":966000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":227000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Saryuun’s diplomatic capital in a fertile river basin, organized around seven court pavilions surrounding Treaty Lake. Cedar balconies, canals, markets, ranger colleges and ward-smithies mix with public assemblies and strict customs halls. River sluices, rooftop archers, tree-root barriers and winged couriers provide layered defense. The city’s seven courts represent different interests rather than a single beastkin bloc.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Principal diplomatic showcase of Saryuun. The seven courts frequently disagree over common law and foreign alliances while remaining within the Concord.

### Emberwatch
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq78tbsm31z","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605473,"nanoseconds":125000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684290,"nanoseconds":328000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Cindervale Marches. Volcanic watch fortress above black ash dunes, breeding ash-wing wyverns and training fire-proof skirmish riders.

**Signature design:** A volcanic cliff fortress made of reddish-black tuff and sloped roofs that shed ash. Wyvern nesting hollows open above refractory stone barracks and heat-shielded cisterns. Fireglass lenses amplify signal fires between remote patrol peaks. Beneath the martial terraces are herders' markets and hot-spring villages. Ash riders can launch quickly in thermal winds, but storms can blind them, and the drakes need feed and rest like any other mounts.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Local weather and old wards hamper foreign armies; the flame-wing squadrons cannot compete in raw strength with adult true dragons.

### West Reception Room
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7axn3yts8","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605468,"nanoseconds":826000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684293,"nanoseconds":670000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Room in Nera's inherited wing where the story begins, containing Ilyra's damaged portrait, the unsigned punishment order, and the aftermath of the fire.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
The fire destroyed curtains and badly damaged Ilyra's final portrait and estate records.

### Anvildeep
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7byaporw2","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605473,"nanoseconds":125000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684290,"nanoseconds":707000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Underground Kharad foundry metropolis beneath the Crownfang ridges. Water lifts, ore roads, bellows halls, ingot banks and reinforced testing galleries produce rune-work, giant ballistae and primitive hand cannons. Artisan guilds guard patents and tolerances; flooding, smoke, powder accidents and supply disputes are constant industrial risks.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Imperial and Veyrath contracts give the hold leverage, but Kharad councils protect proprietary runecraft and sell only selected engines.

### Aelthalas
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7c71vznnk","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605471,"nanoseconds":226000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":935000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Elaranth’s royal capital, grown through five immense living silverwood trunks above the Greenveil mists. Root-level trade terraces rise through artisan groves and hanging gardens to library spirals, royal boughs and great-eagle landing crowns. Living bridges can be coaxed shut, wardwood towers defend the canopy, and court archivists guard treaty histories and sacred timber groves.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Center of Elaranth court politics and the ward-timber monopoly. Sacred groves and high royal districts are tightly restricted to outsiders.

### Nhal-Soryn
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7c8xwpjuq","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605475,"nanoseconds":491000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684290,"nanoseconds":707000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Umbrath’s capital, terraced around a dormant volcanic caldera and dark central lake. Obsidian causeways, ember lanterns, sealwright colleges, markets and the Nine-House Citadel surround the Hall of Resonant Bells. The city is famous for oath-calligraphy, fireglass and Rift-ward politics. Polearm guards, anti-flight mirrors and countersingers protect key districts, while embassies, scholars and political prisoners reflect its contested court life.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Sovereign diplomatic center ruled through shifting Covenant-House coalitions. Its ancient seal machinery requires maintenance and has real limits.

### Vharos
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7d11bp432","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605473,"nanoseconds":547000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":48000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Southern imperial arena city built in a dry red basin on the road to Solenne. The Grand Black Amphitheater, fighter schools, beast pens, debt courts and underground service corridors embody its sanctioned violence, while merchant courtyards, baths, restaurants and noble galleries make it a functioning city beyond the arena. Magistrates and arena guilds regulate spectacles; abolitionists, freed fighters and wealthy patrons contest the same laws. Anti-flying mesh, ward pylons and scorpion batteries contain dangerous beasts.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Future Year-One excursion planned for late autumn, roughly 3–4 days. Students may observe weapon demonstrations, criminal law and the slave economy; specific fights, deaths or confrontations are not predetermined.

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T533 · Saturday 29/10/738] the outer chain-walk holding rows lie past the second bell-post.
<!-- nera-line: {"src":1242,"evidence":[{"order":1241,"id":"msg_mvc9tfqhxhlkgc","revision":0},{"revision":0,"order":1242,"id":"msg_mvc9tuj80c0vzt"}],"sourceRevision":1083,"at":1792235777286,"turn":533,"by":"auto","text":"the outer chain-walk holding rows lie past the second bell-post.","id":"ln_mvjpvwq78tnwi4xq","when":"Saturday 29/10/738"} -->
- [T533 · Saturday 29/10/738] excursion permitted ground is the west basin rim street up to the second bell-post; beyond that post or below the fourth terrace is out of bounds for students.
<!-- nera-line: {"id":"ln_mvjpvwq7wt7lorrj","by":"auto","evidence":[{"id":"msg_mvc9tfqhxhlkgc","order":1241,"revision":0},{"order":1242,"id":"msg_mvc9tuj80c0vzt","revision":0}],"at":1792235777286,"sourceRevision":1083,"when":"Saturday 29/10/738","text":"excursion permitted ground is the west basin rim street up to the second bell-post; beyond that post or below the fourth terrace is out of bounds for students.","turn":533,"src":1242} -->

### Veyrath Townhouse
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7fx8coymq","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605469,"nanoseconds":283000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":48000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
House Veyrath's capital residence overlooking the market bridges.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Used by Nera's wing during the royal reception season and his early meetings with Celestine and Katarina.

### Saltspire and the Seven Harbors
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7i2vpvs34","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605472,"nanoseconds":727000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684293,"nanoseconds":670000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Orsenn Free Cities. A loose network of seven northern harbor fortresses marked by bell towers, beacon chains and mutual-convoy agreements. Stone quays host bolt-galleys and early bronze bombards.

**Signature design:** A cluster of windswept harbor citadels linked by fire-beacon hills and merchant treaties rather than one metropolis. Saltspire's high cliffs shelter a signal-lighthouse and coastal bombards; other treaty harbors contribute warships or withhold them according to commercial votes. Smugglers know tidal caves that no council has admitted exist. Each city's independence makes coordinated blockades possible but never automatic.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Collective naval defense is negotiated by contract; in a trade war one port may stay neutral while another sells guns or escorts.

### Nacre Quay
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7id8mcu26","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605472,"nanoseconds":294000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":964000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Orsenn Free Cities. The largest Orsenn trading port: chained harbor mouth, tidal dry docks, crowded guild exchanges, sea-roc perches and shipboard gun founders.

**Signature design:** A damp, vertical harbor of tar-black piling houses, tiered insurance guild halls, whale-bone cranes and chained stone piers beneath storm bells. Its wealthy live in glass-fronted warehouses, while sailors sleep in tight alleys beneath the seawall. Boarding galleys and sea-roc nesting towers protect the outer sound; power changes hands through contracts, votes and debt collection more often than hereditary succession.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Merchant councils compete; privateer captains and brokers cannot be assumed to represent every Free City.

### Gullhaven
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7iiyeebu6","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605473,"nanoseconds":547000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":589000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Major western imperial harbor at the mouth of the Vael trade waterway. Three harbors—armed merchant basin, fishing/ferry water, and the inspected Crownward Lock—sit beneath sea walls, lighthouse bastions and coastal bombards. Shipwrights, pilots, admirals and customs officials compete for authority amid ropewalks, warehouses and taverns. Fog, reefs and corruption remain real limits despite strong harbor chains and sea-roc couriers.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Likely departure point for the future Crownward Leviathan voyage to Tideglass. Port law and customs still apply to Academy-chartered vessels.

### Kar-Durim
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7k5p5omc0","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605472,"nanoseconds":727000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":91000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Public seat of the Kharad high councils, built around a cliff-sized granite-and-brass gate. Trade terraces conceal deep assembly halls, family workshops, ancestral vaults, counterweight lifts and inward-facing battlements. Rune-gunners and staggered anti-air embrasures make the mountain approaches difficult to bypass.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Council decisions are slow, but a united garrison can hold narrow passes against much larger forces.

### Ravensong Canopy
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7kggnj3o4","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605471,"nanoseconds":226000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":227000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Hidden Elaranth ranger citadel above the Greenveil, built from cedar platforms, hollow-trunk barracks and raptor courts across a misty gorge. Giant hawks and eagles launch through natural wind funnels while leaf-screened anti-air stations cover narrow sky channels. Ground villages supply grain and medicine; the lack of nearby roads limits heavy siege access.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Excellent at controlling forest approaches and aerial channels, but much less dominant on open stone terrain or under sustained heavy artillery.

### Harrowmere
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7kkfk3yub","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":546000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":964000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Fortified logistics city on the Veyrath river plain between Greyford and Thornhold. The **Twelve Wagon Gates** feed granaries, bakeries, veterinary yards, bridge workshops, field kitchens and reserve stables. Harrowmere stores fodder, repair parts and campaign food rather than manufacturing heavy arms. Free farmers, guilds and debt-bound labor all depend on military demand, making requisitions and supply corruption recurring tensions.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Strategic supply municipality governed by ducal quartermasters and civic guilds. Distinct from Forgevault’s metallurgy and Thornhold’s command role; disruption by flood, fire, disease or severed roads can cripple operations.

### Riverside Market
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7kuuz9rw0","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605469,"nanoseconds":811000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":91000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Market district associated with Crownspire student life.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Rhea proposes it as the site of a top-entry first-year gathering after placement results.

### Low drill court
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7kybbry7v","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792165394,"nanoseconds":320000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":91000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T502 · Saturday 22/10/738] flagstone practice court east of the second-year houses, with a rime line across the flags, numbered bays, and a dead ward bench

### Kharad Holds
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7ldadk7pa","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605468,"nanoseconds":826000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":227000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Dwarven territories controlling important mountain roads and foundries.
Geography: a linked but politically independent federation of dwarven stoneholds in the **Crownfang Mountains**, north-east of the imperial heartland and west of Varkesh's high plains. **Kar-Durim** and **Anvildeep** control iron, salt, coal, tunnels, and mountain transit. Dwarven armies favor indomitable shield walls, rune-engineers, armored war-goats, hand cannons, immense artillery, and counter-air bolt batteries rather than numerous flyers. See **Kharad Holdfast Armies**.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Their councils can close passes, condition supplies, and demand prisoner releases.

### Sarthe & Vell
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7prxaxa3e","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792418898,"nanoseconds":0},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":589000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T651 · Friday 4/11/738] counting-house in Vaelcross's second ring, brass plate "Sarthe & Vell, Counting-House, Est. 690"; chest-high oak counter and an inner counting room with a barred window
<!-- nera-line: {"text":"counting-house in Vaelcross's second ring, brass plate \"Sarthe & Vell, Counting-House, Est. 690\"; chest-high oak counter and an inner counting room with a barred window","at":1792418898124,"when":"Friday 4/11/738","turn":651,"evidence":[{"id":"msg_mvf9civqzbr7ri","order":1506,"revision":0},{"revision":0,"order":1507,"id":"msg_mvf9cwymjc0doy"}],"id":"ln_mvjpvwq7pnb1bi4k","src":1507,"sourceRevision":1445,"by":"auto"} -->

### Nine Hollows
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7pspsweap","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605469,"nanoseconds":283000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":470000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Small snowy mining settlement beneath dark fir ridges: roughly forty families, one main street, a company store, chapel, shaft head and ore ropeways. Winter isolation, ration windows and company ledgers make exploitation visible without turning the village into a major city or fortress.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Immediate abuses were exposed, but deeper responsibility for the falsified system remains unresolved within Veyrath administration.
Updates:
- [T157 · winter 735] Immediate abuses were exposed, but deeper responsibility for the falsified system remains unresolved within Veyrath administration.

### Orsenn Free Cities
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7qvcamw6l","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605468,"nanoseconds":826000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684292,"nanoseconds":589000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Northern trading cities dealing in shipping, insurance, mercenaries, and discretion.
Geography: north of the Empire, a cold estuary coast of tidal flats and rival walled ports. Its merchant republics govern themselves separately; **Nacre Quay**, **Saltspire**, and **The Chain of Seven Harbors** control the northern shipping lanes. Orsenn sells convoy protection and mercenary fleets to competitors and is not a united monarchy. Its warships use armored boarding crews, deck scorpions, early bombards, and gull/sea-roc outriders; river defense and seaborne raids matter more than conquering inland fortresses. See **Orsenn War Confederacies**.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Some interests oppose slavery while others finance it indirectly.

### Moonwake
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7u6liec7z","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605471,"nanoseconds":767000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":856000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Elaranth. A ward-protected sea harbor under white limestone bluffs and forest pavilions, exporting selected magical timber, medicines and intricate bowcraft.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Trade convoys travel to western imperial coast and Orsenn; its vessels avoid slave-raiding ports when politically feasible.

### Amberreach
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7ua0t0fq5","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":546000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684290,"nanoseconds":328000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Central imperial grain hub northeast of Vaelcross, built on raised causeways among canals, orchards and broad wheat country. A ring of granary towers and the **Citadel of Measures** store and audit campaign food, while mills, bargemen, tenant farmers and harvest guilds support the civilian economy. Floodable canals and modest fortifications protect the stores. Amberreach matters strategically because control of grain can sustain—or halt—armies without a major battle.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Major civilian and military supply center, not a separate kingdom. Fraud, diverted relief and grain monopolies are plausible conflicts without assuming famine or revolt.

### Crownspire Imperial Academy
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7uyl43tvi","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605470,"nanoseconds":393000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":935000000}} -->
aliases: ["Crownspire"]

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Fortified imperial academy on a high plateau inside Vaelcross. Crownspire combines blackstone walls, anti-air towers, guarded bridges and layered wards with lecture courts, dueling grounds, libraries, student houses, forges, infirmary cloisters and research vaults. It admits 240 students per annual intake across six classes and teaches combat, magic, medicine, artificing, governance and field command. Internal admissions, hearings and protected-student matters fall under Academy charter and wardens rather than arbitrary outside authority.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Nera is a first-year Class A student. Year-One travel anchors include future Vharos, Miravel and Tideglass excursions; these are curriculum plans, not automatically completed events.
Updates:
- [T209 · October 738] Nera is a first-year Class A student. Year-One travel anchors include future Vharos, Miravel and Tideglass excursions; these are curriculum plans, not automatically completed events.

<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7wnn1o4ki","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792302340,"nanoseconds":810000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":470000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T552 · Monday 31/10/738] broad rented chamber over an oil press on Vharos's second terrace, with a good bed, a second cot, table, lamp and brazier, reached by the water stair

### Holy Kingdom of Solenne
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7xi26rqmb","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605470,"nanoseconds":393000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":470000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Southern kingdom housing the Church of the Living Flame.
Geography: south of the Empire beyond the **Sablewater Crossing**, with fertile pilgrimage valleys and white cliff sanctuaries. **Soleris** houses the crown and principal Living Flame cathedral; **Whitewatch** protects a contested crossing. Its army has sanctified foot orders, armored pilgrim knights, lion-drawn chariots, gryphon sentinels, reliquary artillery, and mobile healing/counter-curse priests. The Church's political authority extends beyond its national borders. See **Solenne Sacred Hosts**.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Its Church has international religious influence and charitable institutions but also participates in systems capable of validating abusive contracts.

### Elaranth
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7xrb0sgcy","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605469,"nanoseconds":811000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684290,"nanoseconds":328000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Western elven kingdom that exports exceptionally pure ward timber under an unequal treaty.
Geography: an old western forest kingdom across the **Silverleaf Borderwoods** and the mist-covered **Greenveil River**. The royal capital **Aelthalas**, the treetop stronghold **Ravensong Canopy**, and the harbor **Moonwake** anchor its courts. Elven raptor archers, great-eagle riders, forest cavalry, and living-wood fortifications excel in maneuver and concealment; their heavy siege artillery is comparatively scarce. A hostile treaty and Sylwem's effective hostage status keep diplomacy tense. See **Elaranth Greenhost**.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
A royal child, Sylwem Aelthir, is kept in the Empire as an officially honored exchange student and practical political hostage.

### Blackgorge
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7y5qcr9pv","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605470,"nanoseconds":847000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684289,"nanoseconds":550000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Veyrath frontier fortress-city stacked through a narrow mountain gorge. The **Lower, Middle and Upper Throats** form successive toll, military and command zones linked by switchbacks, cliff galleries, drawbridges and hidden supply routes. Heavy bolt casters, chain nets, anti-air wards and dragon platforms make a direct assault difficult, while army families, traders, embassies, prisoners and shrines make it a lived-in frontier settlement. The eastern road leads toward the Redsteppe, Urzak Gate and Varkesh.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Stores, hospitals, monster stables and high aeries support prolonged defense. The fortress remains vulnerable to starvation, mines, infiltrators, earthquakes, artillery saturation or destroyed aether anchors.

### Greyford
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7yke0i544","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605468,"nanoseconds":446000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684291,"nanoseconds":856000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
River-road transfer town between Harrowmere, Thornhold and the outer mines. Greyford’s bridge, customs fort, counting houses, caravan yards, grain barges and bonded-labor pens make it commercially important despite its modest size. Ledger brokers and transport workshops can redirect supplies as effectively as soldiers. It is also the town where Nera acquired Vesper, and where the Dawnmere family’s grievance against Veyrath retainers began.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Still tied to Vesper’s ownership-to-autonomy history and Alaric Dawnmere’s family grievance; new setting detail does not resolve either.

### Miravel
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7yzfm10mz","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605473,"nanoseconds":547000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684293,"nanoseconds":670000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Independent-minded imperial arcane city built around **nine rival mage towers**, each overseeing a distinct discipline and quarter: barriers, mathematical spellcraft, healing, antiquarian runes, weather, artifice, divination, illusion, and containment. Bridges, rechargeable skywalks, rune markets, libraries and research gardens connect the districts. The Grand Conclave regulates hazardous work and unstable artifacts. Miravel’s politics revolve around patents, teaching rights, subsidies and research rivalry rather than a single unified wizard authority.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Future first-year excursion, planned for late winter to early spring for roughly 4–6 days. Research contests, laboratories, lectures and magical commerce are possible; no disaster or forbidden experiment is predetermined.

### Ashbridge
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq7zgo9jnbw","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605473,"nanoseconds":125000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684290,"nanoseconds":707000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Cindervale Marches. A scorched-stone market and fortress at the southern lava river, where demonkin clans, imperial tax wardens and independent caravan guilds bargain.

**Signature design:** A black-glass trade bridge over a warm sulfur river, surrounded by ash-roof houses, communal bathhouses, reed gardens and two rival customs courts. Imperial colonists, settled demonkin families and caravanners of many kinds live in adjoining wards. The old Cinder Rift stones are protected by separate local custodians, not automatically commanded by any foreign demonkin monarch. Suspicion at the border produces both profitable brokerage and ugly profiling.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Boundary disputes and prejudice complicate military recruitment; not every demonkin community supports the same marchlord.

### Saryuun Concord
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq80qjydgqx","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":966000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":48000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Independent beastkin-led alliance far east and southeast of Varkesh, spanning grasslands, river valleys and stormy coastal cliffs. Seven Charter Courts balance local dynasties, trade houses and clan territories; foxkin, wolfkin, feline, cervine, bearkin, avian-descended families and other peoples have distinct traditions rather than one uniform culture. Silk, rice, herbs, bows, ward-silk and caravan trade support the realm. **Luneshar** and **Skyrake Eyries** are major centers. Saryuun’s existence does not reveal Vesper’s concealed Lan Hsin identity.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Recognized sovereign eastern power divided over imperial trade, refugees and wartime neutrality. Its background is established, but Nera has not automatically visited or dealt with it.

### Silverleaf Borderwoods
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq83h3igdd0","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605471,"nanoseconds":226000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":797000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Elaranth and Aurelian Empire. An ancient oak-and-silverwood frontier west of the Empire, where parallel forest tracks change after fog. Elven ward trees can turn an army aside without a pitched battle; Imperial foresters maintain cut roads, logging forts and signal pyres.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
The unequal Elaranth timber treaty and hostage-like academy diplomacy remain unresolved; disputes here can become border incidents rather than automatically a declared war.

### Whitewatch and Sablewater Crossing
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq848zsd61u","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605472,"nanoseconds":294000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":937000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Fortified pilgrim crossing on the Solenne–Aurelian border. Sanctuary inns and market squares face bridge galleries, toll bastions, radiant scorpions and gryphon watch posts above the Sablewater. Judges handle crossing rights for envoys, caravans and accused travelers, while narrow river flats limit large deployments.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Tolls and religious exemptions create recurring disputes. Either side can close the crossing during a crisis without that automatically becoming a declared war.

### Umbrath Covenant
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq882hilswo","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605475,"nanoseconds":491000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":165000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Independent demonkin realm beyond the Glassfire Fault, centered on volcanic cities, Rift-sealing works and nine competing Covenant Houses. Demonkin are mortal peoples shaped by old Cinder Rift influence, not inherently evil or politically uniform. The Houses elect an Ash Regent while ritual-keepers retain authority over dangerous seal work. Obsidian architecture, hot-water systems, night markets, glasscraft and seal colleges define the region. **Nhal-Soryn** and **Gravefire Gate** are major centers; Cindervale’s imperial demonkin are not automatically Covenant subjects.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Southern rival and trading state with internal political divisions. Border disputes are possible, but no war, demonic invasion or Rift catastrophe is automatically ongoing.

### Vaelcross
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8c8d3hsix","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605469,"nanoseconds":811000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":797000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Imperial capital at the meeting of three rivers, built in seven defensive rings rising from polluted river wards to the palace and Crownspire plateau. Black granite walls, fortified bridges, river-chain gates, mage batteries, gryphon patrols and anti-wyvern towers protect a city of court offices, guild districts, labor wards, temples, markets and noble avenues. The **Three Chains** control barges, **Red Lantern** handles tariffs and debt, **Ten-Thousand Steps** climbs toward court, and **Ashbank** supports heavy industry. Vaelcross is powerful but dependent on maintained wards, crews and supplies.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
The Empire’s main political, financial and religious center. Its layered defenses are formidable but not an invulnerable dome; ward towers and anchors can fail if starved, sabotaged or destroyed.

### Tideglass Isles
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8dnf9ub5l","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":82000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":559000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Region:** Western Glass Sea — offshore imperial archipelago. An island chain of clear blue-green coves, golden tidal terraces, reef villages, wind-carved cliffs, salt markets and ancient lighthouse ruins on a contested sea-lane. Old sea wards can interfere with magical navigation.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
**Future spring Year-One excursion**, 1–2 weeks including the voyage. Students may explore coastal settlements, festivals, ruins and maritime ecology; pirate activity, smuggling discoveries and dangerous storms are options, not settled events.

### Thornhold
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8enhdq8cc","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605470,"nanoseconds":847000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":559000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Veyrath’s fortress capital on a volcanic ridge between two cold rivers. Three terraced basalt rings rise from the labor-and-toll **Lower Chain**, through the barracks and workshops of the **Middle Anvil**, to the ducal **Upper Thorn**. Heavy gates, ballistae, aether projectors, anti-air bolt casters, wyvern patrols, dragon platforms, granaries and cisterns make Thornhold a city built for prolonged defense. Military logistics shape daily life, while bonded labor and strict registry controls expose the harsher side of Veyrath rule. Nera commands his own Blackwing Banner and assigned sectors, never the whole city.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Professional garrisons and hereditary banners rotate through the defenses. Blackwing holds western gatehouses and reserve routes without owning Thornhold. Noble and command districts are well supplied; labor quarters face harsher checkpoints and conditions.

### Vharos bonded registry
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8g5bhmq1g","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792235777,"nanoseconds":611000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":418000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T533 · Saturday 29/10/738] on the lower terrace, reached only past the outer chain-walk holding rows beyond the second bell-post.

<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null} -->
Updates:
- [T531 · Saturday 29/10/738] public desk takes claims 10:00–14:00 on working days and does not open Sundays.

### World Geography
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8ieswoq6s","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791675607,"nanoseconds":739000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":937000000}} -->
always load: yes

#### Notes
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":563} -->
Purpose: Authoritative geography of Asterfall in IY738. Directions are relative to Vaelcross. Named locations retain their detailed cards. New settlements may be introduced without contradicting established geography. Location descriptions do not establish visits or events.

World layout: The Aurelian Empire occupies the central continent; Elaranth lies west, Orsenn north, Kharad Holds northeast, Varkesh east, Saryuun far east/southeast, Solenne south, and Umbrath southeast beyond the volcanic frontier. Distant realms connect through intermediary territories or sea routes.

1. Aurelian Empire — Central Continent
Central imperial power with fertile rivers, western coastlines, southern highways, and eastern military frontiers.

Vaelcross: Imperial capital in the central Three-River Basin; seven defensive rings surround palace, government, and noble districts.

Crownspire Imperial Academy: Fortified high-plateau citadel inside Vaelcross, not a separate city.

Riverside Market: Vaelcross riverfront commercial and student district.

Veyrath Townhouse: Noble residence near Vaelcross's market bridges.

Amberreach: Northeast of Vaelcross, west of the Eastern Marches; fortified agricultural city and army granary.

Miravel: East-southeast of Vaelcross in limestone hills; arcane city of nine competing mage towers.

Vharos: South of Vaelcross on the Solenne road; red-sand basin city of gladiatorial arenas, slave markets, and combat schools.

Gullhaven: Western imperial coast at the Vael waterway's mouth; fortified naval and trade port.

Tideglass Isles: Southwest of Gullhaven in the Western Glass Sea; reefs, fishing settlements, ancient ruins, and Academy maritime excursions aboard the Crownward Leviathan.

2. Veyrath Eastern Marches — Eastern Empire
Semi-autonomous duchy ruled by Duke Cassian Veyrath, extending east of Amberreach toward the Varkesh frontier.

Thornhold: Ducal military capital on a volcanic ridge between two rivers; triple fortress rings and command garrisons.

Veyrath Estate: Fortified ducal residence in Thornhold's defended hinterland.

Blackwing Keep: West of Thornhold atop a gray ridge; headquarters of Nera's 5,000-warrior Blackwing Banner.

Harrowmere: Fertile plain between Greyford and Thornhold; grain stores, cavalry supplies, logistics.

Greyford: Southwestern march entrance at a tributary crossing; customs bridge, caravan trade, bonded-labor yards.

Forgevault: Northeastern iron-mining highlands beyond Thornhold; fortified weapons and armor manufacturing city.

Nine Hollows: Isolated snowy mining valley in outer Veyrath uplands; connected to the mine and forge roads.

Blackgorge: Eastern fortress-city spanning a mountain ravine; principal gateway to Varkesh and the Redsteppe.

3. Cindervale Marches — Southeastern Empire
Volcanic imperial frontier southeast of the heartland, east of the Solenne road and north of Umbrath; ash plains, lava rivers, demonkin settlements, and old wards.

Ashbridge: Southwestern trade crossroads spanning a sulfur river.

Emberwatch: Eastern volcanic cliff fortress near Glassfire; ash-wyvern breeding grounds.

Glassfire Fault: Dangerous southern border separating Cindervale from Umbrath.

4. Elaranth — Western Elven Kingdom
West of the Empire beyond Silverleaf Borderwoods and Greenveil River; ancient forests and living-wood cities.

Aelthalas: Royal capital deep in western Greenveil, among colossal silverwood trees.

Ravensong Canopy: Northwestern forest gorge; hidden ranger fortress and giant-eagle base.

Moonwake: Western coastal harbor beneath limestone cliffs; trades with Orsenn and imperial ports.

Silverleaf Borderwoods: Disputed eastern frontier bordering imperial logging roads.

5. Orsenn Free Cities — Northern Coast
Independent merchant republics along cold estuaries and northern seas.

Nacre Quay: Southeastern estuary coast; largest freeport, banking, shipbuilding, and privateer trade.

Saltspire: Northwestern rocky coast; fortified harbor and cliff lighthouse.

Chain of Seven Harbors: Independent northern coastal ports joined by convoy treaties and signal beacons.

6. Kharad Holds — Northeastern Mountains
Independent dwarven federation in the Crownfang Mountains, northeast of the Empire and west of Varkesh.

Kar-Durim: Principal council city on western mountain approaches; fortified diplomatic and trade passes.

Anvildeep: Southeastern Crownfang interior; vast underground foundry city manufacturing rune machinery and siege engines.

Crownfang Mountains: Snowbound mines, tunnels, fortified passes, and caravan chokepoints.

7. Varkesh Dominion — Eastern Uplands
East of Blackgorge across the Redsteppe; militarized iron-rich highlands, harsh winters, contested grain routes.

Urzak Gate: Western frontier fortress beyond Blackgorge; border customs and caravan control.

Redsteppe: Grasslands and salt flats between Urzak and the Dominion interior.

Kharzun: Eastern upland capital on an iron-rich mesa; foundries, artillery workshops, winter granaries, and military nobility.

8. Saryuun Concord — Far Eastern Beastkin Realm
Sovereign alliance east/southeast of Varkesh, beyond the Namar grasslands toward Dawnwater Gulf; forests, basins, cliffs, and waterways.

Luneshar: Political capital in the central-eastern Lun River basin; Seven Charter Courts, diplomatic palaces, lantern canals.

Skyrake Eyries: Northeastern mountain cliffs; avian city, giant-bird riders, aerial academies.

Namar Grasslands: Western caravan approach connecting Saryuun and Varkesh.

9. Holy Kingdom of Solenne — Southern Continent
South of the Empire beyond the Sablewater River; pilgrimage valleys, vineyards, religious fortresses.

Whitewatch: Northern border fortress facing imperial toll posts.

Sablewater Crossing: Major bridge connecting Solenne and the imperial southern trade road.

Soleris: Southern royal and religious capital in cathedral valleys; Church of the Living Flame, sacred orders, hospitals.

10. Umbrath Covenant — Southern Volcanic Highlands
Independent demonkin realm south of Cindervale across Glassfire Fault, extending toward Solenne borderlands; obsidian mountains, calderas, ancient Rift seals.

Gravefire Gate: Northern frontier fortress controlling passage into Cindervale.

Nhal-Soryn: Southern caldera capital beside a moon-reflecting lake; Nine Covenant Houses and rune colleges.

Glassfire Highlands: Northern mountains with ruined sealing works and volcanic trade passages.

Major travel corridors

Eastern Road: Vaelcross → Amberreach → Greyford → Harrowmere → Thornhold → Blackgorge → Urzak Gate → Varkesh.

Southern Road: Vaelcross → Vharos → Sablewater Crossing → Whitewatch → Soleris.

Ash Road: Central Empire → Ashbridge → Emberwatch/Glassfire → Gravefire Gate → Nhal-Soryn.

Western Road: Vaelcross → Silverleaf Borderwoods → Greenveil → Aelthalas → Moonwake.

Northern Routes: Vaelcross → northern estuaries → Nacre Quay → Saltspire/Seven Harbors.

Mountain Route: Amberreach → Crownfang passes → Kar-Durim → Anvildeep.

Eastern Caravan Route: Varkesh → Namar Grasslands → Luneshar → eastern Saryuun.

Western Sea Route: Gullhaven → Tideglass Isles, Moonwake, and Orsenn harbors.

Geographical rules: Borders, mountains, rivers, coastlines, seasons, checkpoints, and military logistics affect travel. Flying mounts bypass some terrain, not distance, storms, or hostile defenses. Established locations do not move without an explicit story event.

### Veyrath Estate
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8ikitdgiu","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605468,"nanoseconds":446000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":937000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
House Veyrath’s central ducal compound in the Thornhold hinterland: a fortified household with layered gates, armories, granaries, training yards, ward stones, dragon signals and underground supply passages. It contains the family quarters, Nera’s inherited wing, servant spaces and old schoolrooms. The estate serves both frontier defense and Cassian’s internal political control.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Nera’s wing has become a semi-distinct household centered on Isolde, Elise, Liora, Vesper and later Celestine, with loyalties and records increasingly separate from Cassian’s wider household.

### Urzak Gate and the Redsteppe
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8n6zbxsas","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605471,"nanoseconds":767000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684294,"nanoseconds":797000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Chain of wind-battered forts and wells across the Redsteppe grain road between Varkesh and the Veyrath passes. Bear cavalry, sled-chariots, salt caravans and disputed toll stones share open grassland where supply trains are highly exposed. In bad years, control of wells matters more than the tallest fortification.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Customs seizures and seasonal forage shortages create recurring skirmish risk, not an already active full-scale invasion.

### Soleris
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8nbzvbo4w","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605472,"nanoseconds":294000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":559000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Capital of Solenne, built from white limestone basilicas, golden roofs and fortified pilgrimage gates rising toward the Living Flame cathedral. Hospices, libraries, relic foundries and oath courts sit beside royal and Church institutions. Sacred bells coordinate wards and gryphon watches. The same city can shelter captives through charitable orders while other courts ratify deeply unequal service contracts.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Solenne’s institutions can both sanctify coercive contracts and rescue victims; that contradiction is politically important.

### Skyrake Eyries
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8vthw7zcb","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605474,"nanoseconds":966000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":165000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
Vertical Saryuun city built into copper-red cloud cliffs, linked by terraces, bridges, waterfalls and nesting amphitheaters. Hawks, cranes and rare thunder rocs launch from family-controlled ledges. **Cloudquill Bird Archers** and the **Galecrest Roc Guard** protect the skies with agile fliers, wing nets and anti-wyvern weapons, while storms, breeding rights and licensing rivalries shape civilian life.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"canon","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":1} -->
A major Saryuun center whose elders negotiate with Luneshar over military service, bird-breeding rights and eastern mountain access.

### Varkesh Dominion
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq8w6rrqb39","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1791605470,"nanoseconds":393000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684295,"nanoseconds":165000000}} -->

#### Description
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Eastern power controlling iron deposits and upland fortresses while depending on disputed grain routes.
Geography: east of Veyrath's marches across the **Redsteppe** and the contested **Blackgorge Corridor**. The capital **Kharzun** rises above upland furnaces; **Urzak Gate** guards grain caravans and invasion roads. Varkesh's climate and shortages produce bear riders, winter-adapted draft beasts, iron chariots, spear-gunners, storm-wyverns, and mountain guns. Its frontier aristocracy is militarized but its political factions disagree over war. See **Varkesh Ironsteppe Armies**.

#### State
<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"import","kind":"background","cutoff":null,"unavailable":false,"sourceRevision":0} -->
Contains merchants, officers, reformers, and hardliners with differing interests; conflict with the Empire is possible but not predetermined.

## World facts

### Cast roster — Scene Selection Index
<!-- nera-entry: {"id":"lore_mvjpvwq85blz6q1u","draft":false,"createdFrom":"import","createdAt":{"seconds":1792131515,"nanoseconds":20000000},"updatedAt":{"seconds":1792684296,"nanoseconds":705000000}} -->
always load: yes

<!-- nera-section: {"origin":"user","kind":"background","cutoff":null} -->
**Purpose:** Always-loaded scene-selection index. Use importance, location, class/year, duties, relationships, personality, and established schedule to decide which detailed character cards should load. Do not bring characters into a scene merely because they exist.

**Importance rule:** Core characters recur often when relevant to Nera or major plots. Major characters recur around meaningful arcs. Minor characters appear when their specialty, class, duty, club, family, or relationship fits. Background characters normally load only when the current scene directly intersects their role or established location.

**CORE**

- **Nera Veyrath** — Y1 Class A protagonist. Active Academy, combat, relationship, household, political, military and magic scenes.

- **Isolde Veyless** — Y1 Class B; Nera’s bonded maid/bodyguard. Nera’s wing, security, schedules, travel, legal matters and dangerous situations.

- **Vesper Nox** — Y1 Class B; Nera’s foxkin girlfriend. Class B, kitchens, Nera’s wing, private evenings, travel, bargains, wards and registry matters.

- **Liora Fen** — Y1 Class A; Nera’s girlfriend. Class A, meals, ward practice, household scenes, private time and close-group travel.

- **Prince Cedric Aurent** — Y4 imperial prince. Senior politics, royal matters, ceremonies, governance, leadership and Aurent-family scenes.

- **Sylwem Aelthir** — Y1 Class A rank 3; Elaranth royal hostage. Class A, Group 3, wards, archery, technical boards and foreign politics.

- **Lysia Solenne** — Y1 medical track. Infirmary, field medicine, Church issues, audits, injuries, evidence and planned meals with Nera.

- **Lysandra Aurent** — Y1 Class A rank 5; imperial princess. Class A, tower aether halls, duels, field exercises, royal events and planned training/meals.

- **Malfoid Valecourt** — Y1 Class A rank 11; noble crystal duelist. Class A, fencing, noble circles, festivals, rivalry and Academy politics.

- **Fleurdelys Aelwyn** — Y1 Class A elf. Advanced spell practice, sword training, elite first-year exercises, magical theory and high-talent student scenes.

- **Starlia Marin** — 18; no Academy class. Core character from the Vharos arena rescue; Dara Joe’s friend. Vharos, recovery, travel, and scenes involving Nera, Isolde or Dara.

**MAJOR**

- **Dara Joe** — Marin’s friend; red-haired elf and former heavy knight, with no Academy class. Vharos rescue, recovery, protecting Marin and interactions with Nera or Isolde.

- **Magerrett Veyrath** — Y2; Nera’s protective sister. Family, upper-year encounters, defensive magic, Veyrath politics and concern about Nera.

- **Katarina Aurent** — Y3 imperial princess. Royal politics, patrons, the hidden society and formal events; no automatic discovery by outsiders.

- **Celestine Arvayne** — Arvayne heir in Nera’s wing. Household accounts, inheritance, guardianship, creditors and Arvayne history.

- **Alaric Dawnmere** — Y1 Class A rank 1. Class A, Group 3, sword practice, academic competition and Veyrath-conduct disputes.

- **Jeanne d’Arc** — Y1 Solenne healer. Infirmary, aid teams, medical exercises, temple-funded activities and defensive support.

- **Fiammetta Kaorun** — incoming Y1 IY739. Future intake and Saryuun archery; not current IY738 campus attendance.

- **Tanya Rauth** — Crownspire graduate; Blackwing field commander. Military dispatches, Blackwing operations, logistics, engineering and direct orders.

- **Changli Solvair** — Y2. Magical clubs, diplomacy, planning and fire-rune scholarship.

- **Lappland Velsyn** — Y1 wolfkin fighter. Dueling clubs, borderland topics, beastkin circles, risky training and confrontational scenes.

- **Fran Kurofen** — Y1 cat-beastkin swordswoman. Sword society, scholarship circles, food/travel, combat practice and Teacher-related scenes.

- **Okita Souji** — Y1 Saryuun iai swordswoman. Sword society, formal bouts, eastern martial demonstrations and competitive training.

**MINOR**

- **Taigar Veyrath** — incoming Y1 IY739. Family, Veyrath training, Ironroot matters and future Academy preparation.

- **Duke Cassian Veyrath** — Nera’s father. Major family authority, military strategy, reports and Veyrath political decisions.

- **Nessa Quill** — Printing, river wards, contracts, stored messages and student publishing.

- **Evelyne Corven** — Y1 Class A. Commerce, logistics, budgeting and support-device work.

- **Aurelia Fenholt** — Y3. Rescue training, upper-year combat assessments and group coordination.

- **Irene Velmarch** — Y4. Deployment planning, border experience and practical command discussions.

- **Seraphine Duskryn** — Y1. Social events, mirror magic, demonkin circles and student politics.

- **Artoria Pendragon** — Y1. Sword practice, defensive exercises, fair-play disputes and knightly activities.

- **Skadi Morozh** — Y1. Greatsword practice, endurance drills and foreign military-student scenes.

- **Ningguang** — Y1. Economics, negotiation, expedition supply and student investment.

- **Yinlin Veysh** — Y1. Runes, countermagic, demonkin circles and obscure ward work.

- **Sanhua Orren** — incoming Y1 IY739. Future Umbrath intake, anti-magic and ward-defense scenes.

- **Mordred Ashborne** — Y1. Duels, cavalry drills, southern knight circles and competitive scenes.

- **Irelia Senka** — Y3. Sword-and-dance society, upper-year exercises and refugee-defense topics.

- **Teacher — Fran’s Sentient Sword** — With Fran or in artifact, sword-training and magical-relic scenes.

**BACKGROUND**

- **Ilyra Veyrath** — Nera’s memories, old glyphs, family history and hidden magical lineage.

- **Dovan Kirk** — Nera’s training history and technical sword instruction.

- **Hult** — Conditioning, repetition training and Nera’s physical-development history.
